- **2026-11-01**: Snapshot interpolation for shared bodies (#551): `SnapshotInterpolator`, 0.1 s render delay by default. Not wired into the entity layer yet, so the row stays 🚧.
- **2026-11-01**: IM5 names follow interest (#560): a session is sent a name once, when its entity is in view (interest sectors, pins, own avatar); catch-up covers entities that walk in later. Drops stay global (next slice). The IM row stays 🚧.
- **2026-11-01**: Zero-copy data plane persistence is durable by default (#562): the ledger lives in rocksdb under the data dir, `MMO_DATA_PLANE_DB` overrides, `mem://` opts out. Checkpoint throughput is within noise between engines, so the durable default is chosen. Row set to ✅.
- **2026-11-01**: Persistence ledger survives a restart (#557): schema DEFINEs are idempotent, `MMO_DATA_PLANE_DB` opts production into a durable rocksdb URL, restart round-trip test. The zero-copy row stays 🚧: the default is still `mem://`, and a durable default needs a checkpoint-throughput benchmark on rocksdb first.
- **2026-11-01**: Shared bodies drawn from server snapshots in the entity layer (#555), render delay 0.15 s from the lossy-feed sweep. Live-server check still to do.
- **2026-11-01**: Mirror `Interpolator.onPush` listener (#553): a reader can keep more history than the last two ticks. Shared-body wiring in the entity layer is #555, still a draft pending a GIF.
- **2026-11-01**: Merge wave: #523 (RS5 resident talk tests), #527 (RS3 resident baseline, `residents-tick` added to `perf/callgrind-baseline.json`), #536 (editor Wildlife tab, WL2 read-only view; the rig view stays open), #546 (WL2 far LOD: animals past 45 m are one stand-in in the instanced batch, with a 3 m band). WL2 stays 🚧.
- **2026-11-01**: A2 complete on the admin console (#531): `find`, and `set` for Needs, stat bases, Appearance.look and Chunk.lod. A3 complete (#538, #534): cursor targeting in the play view, creature kinds in `spawnlist`.
- **2026-11-01**: M9 lifecycle hook for editor panels (#535). M5 stays 🚧: ability bar and status frame (#537), placeholder ability set (strike, guard, heal); real ability design, damage and healing effects and the live two-account capture are still open. The duel note from #517 is still open too.
- **2026-11-01**: Client physics library (#525, #543, #544): PhysX is the default client backend; server stays on Jolt. ThreadSanitizer gate (#513).
- **2026-11-01**: `addBodies` reports refused bodies (#516). SG2b stays 🚧 (#514): streets are routed along rivers and slopes; civil-engineered roads still open.
- **2026-11-01**: Roadmap status audit and batch: A1, A6, A7 (bots in web workers, #501), EA1, S2, EC1, TE1, TE2 (both rows), SG1, SG2a, SG2c, SG2e, SG3, SG3b, SA1, SG4b (perf run, #505), SG5 (#504), BA1, E5a (#509), E5c, WX1, SG7, SG8, and dungeon physics closed on the evidence each thread checked on main. D4f closed: chests, traps and the boss crystal are entities. E5b is not needed: E5c measures the tick's delta push at 0.23 ms per tick at 1k entities, under 1% of the budget. U4b is 🚧 (#510): recordings load when present, placeholders remain. Duplicate LS1 and EM1 📋 rows and a duplicate SG4b row removed. Physics body pool row added (#515). The zero-copy row stays 🚧: its persistence worker is not closed. IM stays 🚧 until IM7, which is gated on B7.

- **2026-11-01**: SIMD build flags (SIMD1, PR #503): native addons build with fast-math and AVX2 (`-march=haswell`), with bit-pattern finiteness checks and a signed-zero-aware geometry test.
- **2026-10-25**: Paperclip Academic & Systems Improvement Crawler (`scripts/improvement-crawler.mjs`, `npm run crawler:improvements`) + 5 Data & Memory Management implementation patches in [`patches/`](../patches/INDEX.md) (`PATCH-01` AVX2 8-wide FMA3 + 64-bit `aliveBitset` block skipping, `PATCH-02` 32-bit 2D Morton Z-order spatial radix index, `PATCH-03` Dmitry Vyukov bounded lock-free MPSC queue, `PATCH-04` 320-word dirty-bitset SurrealDB group-commit coalescing, `PATCH-05` 32-bit generational handle slab allocator) delegated to **Gemini**.
- **2026-10-25**: Zero-Copy Hybrid Engine Data Plane (`feat/zero-copy-hybrid-data-plane`, 🚧): page-aligned `SharedArrayBuffer` memory arena (`sab-store.ts`, `SOA_LAYOUT`, `OFFSETS`, 64B-aligned Planar SoA columns), 64-byte cache-line padded lockless `AtomicSPSCQueue` with Rigtorp shadow index caching and single-fence batch drain (`lockless-ring.ts`), C++ N-API `stepSimulation`, `applyIntentsAndStep` and 4-wide SSE2 branchless `stepSimulationSoA` kernels (`packages/addons/integrate` / `engine_kernel.node`), 30Hz Simulation Worker (`sim-worker.ts`), `/ws/sim` 4-byte binary WebSocket ingestion gateway (`data-plane-server.ts`), and async Embedded SurrealDB Persistence Worker (`persist-worker.ts`, `persist/schema.surrealql`).
- **2026-10-25**: Park, Replace and Dump buttons on a module's sidecar in the editor's Modules window (#427); fix: Play on a fresh checkout no longer fails on a missing data dir (#428).
- **2026-10-25**: Bug sweep (#417 to #425): engine-core tests no longer depend on `/tmp` fixtures or a starter sword (the engine/game split made both opt-in); terrain-kernel `npm test` builds its JS reference on a clean tree; engine-core builds fortify first; the crafting and dungeon props tests fixed; the Rules editor gets the gather cast time (EG12 follow-up); a crash-looping sidecar runs out of restarts; sidecars can be parked with a debugging dump and replaced fresh (EA11). Every workspace passes locally; CI is still out of credits.

- **2026-10-24**: World scenes (`feat/world-scenes`): `MMO_SCENE` takes a comma-separated list and `world` (every baked `settlement-*` scene plus `places`); one settlement per town for wildlife and raids; a region shard spawns only nodes inside its `REGION_CHUNK_RANGE`; `bake:all` prints `MMO_SCENE=world` and `npm start` sets it when towns are baked. A 3-town bake spawns 2607 entities (385 KB full snapshot) against 962 (142 KB) for one town.
- **2026-10-23**: PL4 (`feat/dev-world-server`): `npm start` boots the world server (embedded SurrealDB under `<data>/world-server`, a random dev `WORLD_API_KEY` per run) before the game server, which registers as `region-1` with `REGION_CHUNK_RANGE` derived from the baked `world_bounds`, so range snapshots reach the world store. `npm start -- --standalone` runs without it.
- **2026-10-23**: E5c (`perf/drop-tick-delta-push`): the core no longer pushes the base64 `tick.delta` / `snapshot` mirror stream that nothing read after E3b; the edge loses its mirror and tick buffers and the `mmo_edge_mirror_*` metrics. B3 push overhead 0.69 → 0.23 ms per tick at 1k entities.
- **2026-10-23**: Players see their gold (`feat/gold-display`, A5, EC1): the gateway sends the wallet balance (0x21) on joining as well as on change; the mirror client gains `onWallet` and `gold`; the client bag shows the balance. Live ops' avatar card gets a Gold button (admin `gold`), the creature grid a `drops.gold` column. Fix: the story editor's rewards kept only `base`, dropping `count` and `gold` on any edit; each reward now has base, count and gold inputs (`patchReward`). A5 done.
- **2026-10-23**: M5 telegraph rendering (🚧, `feat/telegraphs`): the mirror decodes 0x1e into `onTelegraph`; the client draws each warning as a pooled ground decal (circle, cone, rect) that fills over the cast time and fades when it lands or is cancelled. Wildlife warning cones now show.
- **2026-10-23**: A2/A3 debugger entity actions (`feat/debugger-entity-actions`): the play-mode selection card edits `set`-whitelisted fields inline (Enter/blur sends, the answer shows per field; card "Intent" maps to `PlayerIntent`) and gains server inspect, heal, teleport-me-here and despawn (refusals shown). `help` now lists `appearance`.
- **2026-10-23**: U3 needs death loop fixed (`fix/needs-death-loop`): a respawn fills hunger and thirst (NeedsSystem refills a body that rises from 0 Health), and feet within `drink.reachM` of a water surface refill thirst at `drink.perSecond` (`content/rules/needs.json`). Food (consumables, a use op) is the follow-up.
- **2026-10-23**: M5 duel outcomes (`fix/duel-outcomes`): `DeathSystem` ends a duel by first blood (`checkBlood`) when a duelist is felled by their opponent (`wasDuelDeath`) and puts the loser right with no death penalty; the gateway calls `duels.forget` on disconnect, logout, eviction and handoff, so a duel cannot outlive a player. Both ends go out on 0x20.
- **2026-10-23**: Crafting fix (`fix/crafting-pouch`): crafts could never succeed, because `CraftingService` read its own in-memory pouch that nothing filled. It now counts and spends the gathering pouch (`GatheringSystem.take`, written through to `gathering.db`, so PL3b's world sync carries it); the dead pouch is gone. Fleet crafts still debit only after the world commits. Follow-up: a client crafting window and a `CraftMessage` in the protocol.
- **2026-10-23**: IM8 cross-region view at borders (`feat/im8-border-view`, protocol v27): a player within 96 m of a live neighbour region gets a `border` message (0x26) with a 60 s ticket signed with the fleet's `WORLD_API_KEY`; the client opens a read-only observer socket to the neighbour (hello `observe`), reports where it stands (clamped into its home region), receives the neighbour's DeltaSets for that spot in a second mirror, and draws them in their own entity layer; past 128 m it is told to close. Neighbours come from the registry the migration coordinator already reads.
- **2026-10-23**: IM6 (`feat/im6-all-mode`): interest management is on by default (`MMO_INTEREST=0` turns it off); welcome, resync and periodic snapshots are interest snapshots (full replaces of the session's sectors, shared per tick, since IM3). Admins can ask for everything (`interest {mode: all|near}` admin command); the editor's play client starts in `all` and the debugger's new interest panel toggles "show what the player sees" and maps the sectors around the avatar. Dungeon instances run their own gateway without interest, so they still see everything.
- **2026-10-23**: IM5 pins and events (`feat/im5-pins-events`): with interest on, a session's party members in the region and its foes (10 s after a blow either way) ride a pinned entry wherever they are, and hits, life, ability, telegraph and status events reach a session only when an entity they name is in its sectors or pinned. Not yet: quest NPC pins and `farVisible` entities (no tag exists); names and drops stay region-wide (cheap, and the client keeps names across leaving view).
- **2026-10-23**: IM4 edge assembly (`feat/im4-edge-assembly`): through the edge the core sends one `wire.sectors` push per tick (each needed sector's bytes once, plus every session's plan of entries) and `game-relay.ts` assembles each player's DeltaSet from the shared buffers. B7 channel bytes with interest: 2.1 MiB to 451 KiB per tick at 5k towns, 333 to 228 KiB at 1k spread; edge assembly 0.7 to 1.6 ms per tick for 50 sessions.
- **2026-10-23**: IM3 interest in the core (`feat/im3-interest`, `MMO_INTEREST=1`): `net/interest.ts` cuts each tick into per-sector Deltas (moves in arrive in full, moves out are listed as despawned there; real spawns and despawns ride the global sector), encoded lazily once per tick and shared; each session sees the sectors within 96 m (dropped past 128 m, `content/rules/interest.json`) as one DeltaSet, with full states for sectors it enters and a full replace on welcome, resync and snapshot ticks. B7: 55.4 to 4.9 KiB per client per tick at 1k spread, 274.6 to 10.5 KiB at 5k, 32 KiB at 5k towns; per-session sends cost tick time until the edge assembles (IM4).
- **2026-10-23**: IM2 `DeltaSet` (`feat/im2-deltaset`, protocol v26): 0x25 carries one tick as length-prefixed sector entries (delta, full, leave, pinned); the mirror applies a set atomically (leave, despawns, then upserts, re-homing each entity; one interpolator push) and tells left-view from despawned; the client skips the spawn portal for entities that only came into view. The gateway sends each tick as a one-entry set.
- **2026-10-23**: IM1 one publish per tick (`feat/im1-one-publish`): while the delta is global every session shares one region topic, so the channel carries one copy per tick instead of one per occupied sector. B7: channel bytes 3.5 MiB to 98 KiB per tick at 1k spread, 17.2 MiB to 472 KiB at 5k; core tick 9.8 to 2.0 ms at 1k, 64 to 8.2 ms at 5k.
- **2026-10-23**: IM0 B7 interest bench (`feat/im0-bench`): `test/bench/b7-interest.mjs`, 50 sessions through `ChannelTransport` over spread and towns layouts. Baseline: 55.3 KiB per client per tick at 1k and 274.6 KiB at 5k; the channel carries 3.5 MiB per tick at 1k spread (one copy per occupied sector) and 17.2 MiB at 5k.
- **2026-10-23**: IM interest management spec (📋, `docs/interest-management-design`): every client gets the whole region delta today (73.8 KiB/tick at 1k, B4), and through the edge each occupied sector topic carries its own copy. Design: per-sector deltas encoded once, a `DeltaSet` per client assembled at the edge, pins, interest snapshots; B7 bench; slices IM0–IM7.
- **2026-10-23**: E3b client cutover (`feat/edge-cutover`): players reach the core through the edge. The core's gateway serves its port over the channel (`ChannelTransport`) with `MMO_EDGE=1`, and the edge's `EDGE_GAME_PORT` relays WebSocket frames, topic publishes and HTTP (PCG downloads, `/health`). `npm start` runs the region with no player port and the edge on 7777, so the client is unchanged. The SIMD build flags and docs on the old `feat/edge-core-split` branch are left there for their own PRs.
- **2026-10-23**: PL3c (`feat/social-world-records`), the last PL3 slice: guilds, parties, friends and blocks are world records (`social_record`, `PutSocialRecord`/`GetSocialRecords`/`AllocateSocialIds`). `SocialWorldSync` seeds the region's stores before the books read them, hands the gateway tracked stores whose writes go uphill on the save timer (409: the slower region's edit is dropped and reread), and refreshes every 60 s (books reload). Decided: world-assigned ids from 1,000,000,000; a region's own guilds and parties are renumbered once at its first fleet boot.
- **2026-10-23**: PL3b (`feat/character-extras`): a character's quest log and materials pouch ride its world record as `extras` (`CharacterExtrasView`), seeded into `quests.db` and `gathering.db` at login (the pouch cache is dropped), so they follow it to any region. A store a region doesn't run keeps the world's last copy. Friends and blocks moved to PL3c as world records (blocks are per account; friend lists are read across players).
- **2026-10-23**: PL3 spec approved (regions as hot caches: place state in the range snapshot, per-character state in the character record, guilds and parties as world records). PL3a (`feat/region-places`): resource node stocks ride each range snapshot as `places`; the upload's skip check covers them, so a gather alone still ships, and recall seeds `gathering.db` from the world copy before the gathering system opens it. Dungeon pool rebaked with DF1–DF3: 30 dungeons, same layouts and shells per seed, about 690 props each (was 157); the old pool is kept at `data/dungeons.bak-2026-10-23`.
- **2026-10-23**: PL2 embedded SurrealDB (`feat/world-embedded-surreal`): the world server runs SurrealDB in process (`@surrealdb/node`, RocksDB under its data dir) by default. Before, it connected to a separate server over `ws://` or, unset, used a plain in-memory JS store. The host report, atomic, migration and state-plane store contracts now run on embedded SurrealDB in `npm test` instead of skipping; the suite runs with `--test-force-exit` because the embedded engine keeps Node alive after `close()`.
- **2026-10-23**: PL1 world ingestion (`fix/world-body-limit`): the webrpc router parses its own bodies with caps from the schema (snapshot 4 MiB, ledger 1 MiB, character state 256 KiB). The server and the ten test apps no longer put a 256 KB parser in front of it. Snapshot uploads come due again: the region passes the 300-tick upload interval, not its 1 s save interval, and a fresher save keeps the pending slot. New PL2 (embedded SurrealDB) and PL3 (regions as hot caches) on the list.
- **2026-10-23**: DF2 and DF3 land on main (`land/dungeon-furnishing`): #347 and #349 had merged into their stack branches, not main, so main had only DF1's arrangements. Also clears conflict markers committed in `.gitignore`, keeping every entry (valgrind `/perf/`, the licensed-art locals, the dungeon art overlay). The pool still needs a rebake to show DF1 and DF3.
- **2026-10-22**: DF3 dungeon furnishing C (`feat/dungeon-furnishing-density`, stacked on #347): dense built-in rooms (crypt, barracks, armoury, library, chapel, jail, mess hall, ...) from new patterns, item reach, a per-room fill and 33 more prop kinds.
- **2026-10-22**: DF2 dungeon furnishing B (`feat/dungeon-furnishing-look`, stacked on #346): procedural meshes for every dungeon prop kind served from the run's Pcg library, prop colour slots in the looks, and a local art overlay (`dungeon:art`) that maps kinds to catalog models by whole-word names.
- **2026-10-22**: DF1 dungeon furnishing A (`feat/dungeon-furnishing`): dressing content lines can name an arrangement (row, grid, flank, beside, ring) and the built-in rooms use them; scatter-only dressing is byte-identical, layouts and shells unchanged. Rebake the pool to see it.
- **2026-10-22**: Build repair: `npm run build` on main failed after the arlo merges. `@mmo-engine/art` had lost `export * from "./media/index.js"` (Phase 5 conflict resolution), the editor's dialogue tab and three module editor panels (hud, flow, story) imported files that moved or never existed at those paths, the database workspace and a test had type errors, and `modules/template-native` had no `binding.gyp` or sources so `build:native` failed. All restored; the root build exits 0. No row change.
- **2026-10-20**: Dungeon look (D4c + D4e, ✅, stacked on #337): dark-fantasy lighting and muted matte materials for dungeon runs from per-theme data (`content/dungeons/looks.json`, sent once as a `look` message): no sky or weather, near-black fog, dim ambient, flat-shaded matte stone by material slot, budgeted flickering torchlight. Palette and light numbers are untuned. Enemy models from data with the stand-in fallback (EM1), the loading screen (LS1) and dungeon resource nodes (RN1) are on the list.
- **2026-10-20**: Dungeon props (D4d, ✅, stacked on #336): baked pillars, furniture and torches now stand in dungeon runs through the world's spawn-table path (`content/dungeons/props.json`, `buildSpawn`, `spawnScatterEntity`); wall system skips static bodies; lights, chests/traps as entities and decals are D4e to D4g.
- **2026-10-20**: Dungeon shell on the client (D4b, ✅): dungeon runs showed only a stand-in floor because the baked shell never reached the client; it now goes through the existing PCG asset path (prebaked-mesh library asset, one static `AssetRef` entity, the Pcg service on the instance gateway) and the client's Pcg client follows the gateway it joined. Colours per theme are D4c.
- **2026-10-20**: SG2b vertical profile routing built (PR #335, `feat/roads-grading`): `routeEdge`/`routeRoads` A* over a corridor-bounded heightfield grid mirroring `planPlaces`'s `walkRoute` — water/sea/ice cells and cells steeper than a cart grade (tan(8°)≈0.08) blocked at the node, 8-connected no-corner-cut, Douglas-Peucker simplify, straight-spine fallback when no gradeable route exists within the detour budget. 6 tests green (no road enters water; cliffs are routed around, not carved). `tsc` clean. Client renderer + settlement wiring remain follow-ups.
- **2026-10-19**: Build speed (BLD1, ✅): `npm run build` builds each workspace once in dependency order (was 197 `tsc` runs, schema alone 22x) and every tsconfig is `incremental`; TypeScript-only build 1521 s to 131 s (117 s warm).
- **2026-10-19**: Build fix: the editor's `build:deps` now builds `@mmo-engine/build` first. A fresh clone's `npm run build` failed in the editor (8 TS2307 errors in `build-host.ts`, `build-profiles.ts`, `files-host.ts`, `host.ts`) because that package's `dist` only existed where it had been built before; no row change.
- **2026-10-19**: Test repair: the gateway tests (frame cap, ingress and pickup rate limits) asserted ws-library details (close code 1009, every excess message counted) that uWebSockets, the default transport, does not share, and the server-modules tests still modelled the pre-modularity core that built every system; both now check what they were written to protect and pass on both transports. The 9 red tests on main were these, not engine bugs.
- **2026-10-19**: Settlement count (SG6): `bake:all --sites max` is the default. SG7 homesteads and SG8 roadside places (shacks, camp sites, guard outposts, inns only beside an outpost) planned.
- **2026-10-19**: SG7/SG8 scenes: `places-scene.ts` and `places.scene.json` build every planned place as a Structure node (farmhouse with field or pen, outposts, inns, shacks, camp sites).
- **2026-10-19**: SG7/SG8 first slice: `packages/settlement/src/places.ts` plans routes between the towns and the places along and between them; `npm run settlement:places`; `bake:all` step `places`.
- **2026-10-19**: World generation (WG5): pangaea shape (`--shape=pangaea`, 71.6% land, one supercontinent). Town bake on the continents world: 8 sites found, 2 on each continent (the 4 km island world gave 2 to 3).
- **2026-10-19**: World generation (WG4): continents. `land.coast.shape` "continents" gives separate large landmasses, and `bake-world` takes `--shape=island|peninsula|continents` (continents grow the world to at least 8 km a side). 70% land on the 8 km world (continents joined by land bridges).
- **2026-10-19**: World generation (WG3): island topology. `land.coast.shape` "island" puts one main island in open sea with optional satellites; the default world uses it (73.5% land, spawn in plains). Peninsula remains the schema default.
- **2026-10-18**: World generation (WG1, WG2): the default coast gives about 74% land on the 4 km world (`coast.waterEdge` 0.4, was 0.6 at 52%), and a global humidity dial (`climate.humidity`) scales rain and land moisture. Islands (WG3) are next.
- **2026-10-18**: Designer access (1g) EG1 to EG14 done (✅, PRs #292 to #305): dungeon run rules and entrances, combat and body, crafting and town rules as forms; movement, combat resolution, creature brains, skill pacing, social limits, gathering regrow and dungeon wall/bot pacing moved from code into validated data with forms; VFX budgets now read by the client and editable; persistence settings and day cycle/bots as Live ops › Server settings (env vars still win). Everything hot-reloads except what the server reads at start.
- **2026-10-18**: Designer access (1g) added: EG1 to EG14 from the editor gap audit (#290), which found dungeon run rules and most of crafting with no editor panel, combat, movement, creature and skill constants held in code, and combat and body rules reachable only as a raw tree.
- **2026-10-17**: SG5 settlement town plan (🚧, PR #287): towns had taverns and bakeries scattered through housing, scattered craft and farm blocks, no wall, and wall/gate/archway models used as houses. Now: a town square with a well where the main roads cross; civic, market, entertainment and education quarters grown from it and sized by the buildings they must hold (one school and one tavern in a village; halls, theatre, library, barracks and cathedral only above a population); church life (parish church, rectory, churchyard, almshouse-hospital, monastery, apothecary); crafts and farms outside along the roads; buildings facing their streets; a towered ring wall with four walk-through gates for towns of 45+ households; wall-type models kept out of the building kinds. Rules `settlement-rules/4`, golden hash re-pinned; 60 to 4000 people x 8 seeds all generate. [Settlements](SETTLEMENTS.md) sums up the history and the next steps.
- **2026-10-16**: WX2 incremental, content-addressed world bake (✅): the survey is content-addressed by a sha256 of the canonical spec JSON (skipped when unchanged); tiles are keyed by `(seed, cx, cz, lod, terrainVersion, surveyHash)` in a `terrain_tile_keys` table reusing `heightsFingerprint()`; `bake:world` gains `--region rx,rz` and `--survey-only` with the per-chunk tile loop parallelized across worker threads. `TerrainCacheOptions` gained optional `minChunk`/`sizeChunks` (needed by `invalidateRegion`); `terrainVersion` is optional and defaults to 1 (the bake passes `procgen.terrainVersion`, a deterministic `fnv1a32` of the generator version + amplitude + frequency). A regional re-bake re-runs the survey (it needs it for tile generation), calls `invalidateRegion` to drop that region's tiles, then regenerates only that region's chunks — the other regions' tiles are untouched. The `--force` overwrite gate deletes `world.db` + WAL sidecars ONLY on a forced full bake; incremental mode (`--region`/`--survey-only`) updates the world in place and never deletes it. LOD 0 tiles go through `terrain_tiles` (via `TerrainTileStore`); LOD > 0 tiles go through a separate `terrain_tile_data` table, so the existing `TerrainTileStore` API is unchanged.
- **2026-10-16**: WX1 weather as a field — climate sourcing (🚧): the sim no longer hardcodes its climate. `FieldService` takes a `ClimateParams` block (wind vector, sea temp, lapse rate, dry distance, orographic lift, recycling) and `world-canvas.tsx` reads it from the welcome's `WorldInfo` via an additive cast, falling back to `DEFAULT_CLIMATE` (which mirrors `default.world.json`) until the server sends a `climate` block. The two field-service tests are now real (climatology sanity + `sample()`/`step()` evolution) instead of skipping. Remaining couplings: wet look, ripples, weather audio.
- **2026-10-15**: Cell shading is a component (`feat/shading-components`): the client's `render/shading/` holds named shading components (`toon` = the cel look, `standard` = three's own PBR) and applies whichever one a *kind of thing* carries — `model` (an art-pack or PICG model's own material), `placeholder` (a stand-in shape), `prefab` (a composed entity). Which kind carries what is content (`content/materials/materials.json` -> `shading`, validated in `packages/art`, applied by `applyGameContent`), so turning cell shading on for something is adding the `toon` component to its kind there — never editing a renderer. Every kind is toon by default (the look's numbers stay in the `toon` block; `shading.rows` gives each kind its ramp row). Modular characters keep the character kit's own style. `applyToon` also no longer assumes the material already has an `onBeforeCompile` patch.

- **2026-10-15**: WL2 megafauna and megaflora (🚧, `feat/megafauna`): tier 5 bosses in the wildlife table (15 prehistoric species, rarity <= 2 per 100 km2, world cap 4, health 3 to 8 trolls), raid rules per boss, `planMegafauna`/`raidTargets`/`bossModels`, megaflora ecology layers; data, schema, planner and docs only.
- **2026-10-15**: BA1 `npm run bake:all` (🚧, `feat/bake-all`): `scripts/bake-all.mjs` runs build, world, loot, dungeons, settlement (best sites first, `--auto-size` falls back 600/300/100), scale-fit and the wildlife plan and models in order, skipping what exists (`--force` rebuilds), with `--only`/`--skip`/`--dry-run`, per-step timing, a stop at the first failure naming the command to rerun, and the `MMO_*` block to start the server (also in `<content>/bake-env.local.txt`, gitignored). One paths module resolves flag, then `MMO_*` variable, then default once for every step; `settlement:bake`, `scale-fit` and `scale-report` now follow `MMO_CONTENT_DIR`, `MMO_SETTLEMENT_OUT`, `MMO_SCENES_DIR`, `MMO_CATALOG`, `MMO_SCALE_OUT` and take `--scenes <dir>`; the server reads `MMO_SCENES_DIR` (alias), `MMO_CATALOG` and `MMO_ASSETS_DIR`.
- **2026-10-15**: WL2 creature brains (🚧, `feat/creature-brains`): the wildlife table spawns real creatures (flee, defend, territorial warning on the telegraph, pack flank, day/night activity, aquatic swimming, instinct raids), natural-weapon bases, settlement centres into the planner, animal model by asset hash. Fixes `MMO_WILD_CREATURES=1` crashing at boot (the creature system was asked for before it was assembled).
- **2026-10-18**: SG2e green squares (`feat/green-squares`): settlement sites and farm fields read from the survey's green land; the "too wet" failure on the default world (farm fields reaching the sea) is gone. Wet-site hunting replaced by squares, size from area, farms capped to the land.
- **2026-10-15**: SG2d sites above the water (`fix/site-above-water`): on the default world the site at (400, 920) stood 1 m over the sea and 126 of its 128 structures were refused by the spawner (survey sites ignored the stored tiles' detail noise and the 1 m wet-footing margin). `rules.sites.minElevationM` (4 m) now applies to the footprint's lowest ground and to the water around it, never below `STRUCTURE_MARGIN_M` + `detailAmplitude`; the world's `plains.coast.marginM` is 5 m (re-bake). Zero refusals at 100, 300 and 600 people.
- **2026-10-15**: WL1 wildlife import (🚧, `feat/wildlife-import`): GLB pack importer (`import:glb`, natural-size rescale, licence kept in the catalog), `wildlife:plan` name-pattern ecology layers incl. sea-floor water habitat, farm dressing patterns, `content/credits.json` (CC-BY wording marked to confirm). Static placement only. Wildlife table and planner: trophic ratios, danger rings, safe radius, `docs/WILDLIFE.md`.
- **2026-10-15**: WL2 procedural animal movement (🚧, `feat/procedural-animals`): static animal GLBs get a generated seven-bone skin and a speed-driven gait (idle, walk, trot, gallop), an attack windup/strike and a death fall, in the client (`render/animal/`). Option C chosen over using or driving the procedural-animals library (MIT); no code vendored. Pure parts unit-tested; shown on real imported animals through a scripted mirrored entity (no server creature uses it yet).

- **2026-10-15**: TE2 sea level in placement (🚧) on `fix/sea-level-placement`: houses, trees and props were placed below the water (on the 4 km scratch world, 131 of 9 504 sampled ecology points were land layers on ground at or below sea level, and 136 to 230 of 288 buildings stood wet at shore origins). `habitat` (land default, shore, water with a depth range) on ecology layers and spawn-table archetypes, land needs 0.3 m over the water (sea level is the floor even where the lake lookup finds none), the scene spawner refuses buildings under 1 m above it or on rivers and wet props, wet settlement parcels are dropped and the grid grows to compensate. The wilds plan's hash changes (reeds/lilies annotated): re-bake.

- **2026-10-15**: TE2 plains and terraces (🚧, `feat/terrain-plains`): the default world had no site at 3 m of relief for 100, 300 or 600 people (land within 0-30 m of the sea and under 2 degrees: 14%). New opt-in `plains` pass (terraces, ramps, rolling undulation, river and shore flats, fades out above 45-100 m) and `reachableLand`; `rules.sites` gains `walkSlopeDeg`/`requireReachable` and `TerrainFields.spawn`, so a plateau ringed by cliffs is rejected. Default world opts in (re-bake): that land share 53%, strict sites 2 / 2 / 1 for 100 / 300 / 600, nearest under 500 m from the spawn. Survey +6%.
- **2026-10-15**: TE1 terrain relief (🚧) on `feat/terrain-erosion`: mountains read as blobs on dirt mounds (rounded quartic range profile, ridge noise only scaled the height, river cuts capped near 24 m, no bare-rock angle, volcano cone left perfectly smooth). New opt-in passes: `ranges.crest` (pointed profile + ridged multifractal), `erosion.incision` (implicit stream power), `erosion.rockTalusDeg`, `volcano.gullies`; the default world opts in (re-bake needed, town sites move). Survey 9.6 s to 13.5 s.

- **2026-10-15**: GS2 global scale applied (🚧): `loadScale` at boot, the factor threaded through `spawnScatterEntity` (so scene, scatter, ecology, formation and admin spawns all agree between Transform and BodyConfig); categories on ecology layers and spawn-table archetypes; the client draws a stand-in at its body size, no longer multiplying the Transform scale on top.
- **2026-10-15**: PH1 prop colliders (🚧): `propShape` derives a prop's box/sphere from its model bounds (art catalog, PICG cook) x scale at spawn; ecology layers and the forest table use `fit` instead of fixed boxes (a 25 m pine was a 5 m box, birches had a unit box); props under 0.25 m are stepped over. The Jolt body, nav obstacle and PropCollisionSystem all read the one BodyConfig. `makeWorldPopulator` is now async (it cooks the PICG trees for their sizes).
- **2026-10-15**: Dungeon physics (🚧, `feat/physics-dungeons`): `DungeonWallSystem` sweeps each axis cell by cell (diagonal corner clips and thin walls at speed are refused; a move over 8 m a tick is a teleport), dressing `collision` and baked `colliders` block bodies, spawns clear of colliders, walkable grid and shell checked equal.
- **2026-10-15**: Settlements follow the global scale (`feat/scale-settlements`): `scale` on the request scales parcels and streets (streets at least 2 m), the manifest records it, the scene stays authored at scale 1 with margin-aware footprints, `settlement:scale-report` flags models that overflow their parcel; golden hash re-pinned.
- **2026-10-15**: SA1 architectural scale (🚧, `feat/scale-architecture`): settlement archetypes get `floors`/`storeyM` and the rules an `architecture` block (human 1.8 m, storey 2.7 m, ground ceiling 2.44 m, roof 2 m); `buildingHeightM` replaces `heightM` in the scene; building factor 1; `settlement:scale-fit` writes per-model factors to the gitignored `content/scale.local.json` (`MMO_SCALE`); the editor gauge only counts whole-building models. Rules version bumped to settlement-rules/2, golden hash re-pinned.
- **2026-10-15**: settlement CLIs resolve typed paths against where npm was run (`INIT_CWD`), so `--catalog assets/catalog.json` and `--out content/...` work from the repo root (`fix/settlement-cli-paths`).
- **2026-10-15**: SG2c flat-land settlements (🚧, `feat/settlement-flatland`): `rules.sites` gains `maxElevationM`, `lowlandBandM`, `maxReliefM`, `ridgeHeightM`, `mountainClearanceM`; `selectSites(..., extentM)` measures relief over the town's footprint from the height field (a long gentle ramp passes slope but fails relief) and sites report `elevationM`/`reliefM`; `Structure` spawning stands on the highest ground under its footprint with a foundation slab to the lowest. No rules-version bump (manifests unchanged, golden hash kept).
- **2026-10-15**: EA30 Scale view: edit content/scale.json in the editor (factors, per-asset rows, live validation, undo, Save/Revert), Problems and Details entries, and a gauge sizing the local art catalog beside a 1.8 m character with sane-band flags per category. Also restores App.tsx, whose valgrind merge left stray Tab/TABS and main/footer blocks that broke the editor build.
- **2026-10-15**: SG4a settlement art (🚧): `Structure` takes an optional `asset`, `buildArtMap` maps the 12 kinds to the packs' building presets by name pattern, `settlement:bake --catalog` bakes it into the (gitignored) scene. Played in the client: 288 buildings of 12 kinds with their models, and as stand-in boxes without. The spawner box is now centred on the ground.
- **2026-10-15**: Structure blocking proved and `Collider` spawned: an integration test walks a real kinematic character into scene-spawned Structure boxes from eight directions at three yaws (never inside, never a gap), over them (Y interval), through the 2 m street between adjacent parcels, and after a second `spawnSceneOnBoot`; a settlement test asserts the footprints never overlap and every street is wider than a capsule. Scene `Collider` nodes now spawn (box/sphere as a static body, `none` walk-through, or an override of a prop prefab's own collision).
- **2026-10-15**: SG4b movers vs baked statics (🚧): crowd agents path round and never end inside Structure boxes (yawed too); creatures share the players' capsule path; `clearOfStatics` keeps player spawn, respawn, admin teleport, saved-position restore and creature spawns out of buildings (the world spawn sits inside the generated town); ground items confirmed non-blocking. Per-tick cost unchanged (valgrind not available on Windows).
- **2026-10-15**: SG2a settlement site selection (🚧): `selectSites` scores each survey cell by slope, water distance and fertility and keeps the best apart; on the shipped world it finds two sites in 64 ms (the survey itself takes 8.5 s). `settlement:bake --world --site` places the town there. Also fixed the CLI's default output folders, which resolved to `packages/content` instead of `content`.
- **2026-10-15**: SG3b settlement occupancy (🚧): the occupancy baker deals a 600-person town into 136 households and homes, 283 jobs and 86 shops, with the demographic cohorts and classes exact; three new gates (population, homes, jobs); the golden hash is re-pinned for the new manifest section. Residents are data only for now; spawning them needs a simulated-agent path.
- **2026-10-15**: SG3 settlements spawn through the scene spawner (🚧): a `Structure` scene component (kind and footprint), a spawner branch that makes it a static box with the kind's asset hash (art mapping comes later), `settlementScene` and `settlement:bake --scene --origin`. A baked 600-person town spawned as 288 entities of 12 kinds around the chosen origin. Occupancy and NPCs split out as SG3b.
- **2026-10-15**: SG1 settlement generator first slice (🚧) on `feat/settlement-engine-s1`: `@mmo-engine/settlement` bakes one deterministic town (demographics, demand, street grid, four districts, parcels, WFC-placed structures from 12 archetypes) to `<id>.settlement.json` with seven validation gates, a verbosity-filtered event log and a content hash pinned by a golden test; `npm run settlement:bake`. Rules are data (`SettlementRulesSchema`). Roadmap rows SG2 to SG4 added for what follows.

Mitigation next.

- **2026-10-04**: U4 audio: `AudioEngine` (Web Audio buses, unlocks on first gesture, pauses with the tab), synthesized cues and an audio section in settings (master, combat, interface, ambience, mute; persisted). (#106)

- **2026-10-04**: U6 chat reply: `/r` and `/reply` whisper the last character who whispered you; whispers read from/to and are tinted. (#95)

- **2026-10-04**: S3b party persistence: `SqlitePartyStore` (`parties.db`); a restart no longer disbands parties. (#102)
- **2026-10-03**: S2c friends and blocks persistence: `SqliteSocialStore` (`social.db`) behind `GatewayOptions.socialStore`, write-through from `FriendBook` and `BlockList`. Friends lists and blocks now survive restarts.
- **2026-10-03**: Roadmap brought up to date: an "At a glance" summary; U3, U10, U12 and M2 marked in progress, U5, C3 and L3 built; the update log put back in date order under its heading. README layout lists the editor, edge and every package.
- **2026-10-03**: Valgrind without Docker: `npm run valgrind:memcheck` and `valgrind:perf` call `memcheck.mjs` and `perf.mjs` directly again on the host's valgrind; the Docker launcher (`launch.mjs`, `Dockerfile`, `docker-run.sh`) is gone, replacing the container repair below. On Windows, run them from WSL.
- **2026-10-03**: Edge/core split E1 edge skeleton: new `apps/edge` process (Express `GET /health`, client WebSocket) routes packets by type from strictly validated `content/edge/routes.json` to local modules (`ping`) or the core; the core channel is a stub until E2, so core-bound packets answer `core.unavailable` (the contract E4 reuses). Graceful SIGTERM/SIGINT. Valgrind not run (skipped at the user's request).
- **2026-10-03**: Valgrind repaired: the container run (`docker-run.sh`) rebuilt every addon from scratch on every run, silently (Jolt takes minutes), so `npm run valgrind:*` looked hung, and two runs at once corrupted the shared volume. Addons now rebuild only when their sources change (hash in the volume), runs are serialised with `flock`, progress is printed and a failed addon build shows its log. Verified: `valgrind:memcheck -- physics` clean in 16 s, `valgrind:perf` all 8 workloads in 84 s (container Node is v25; the baseline is from v22, so deltas are indicative only). Valgrind/perf are no longer paused.
- **2026-10-03**: M3c combat skills: the attacker's weapon skill (Cutting/Thrusting/Bludgeoning, via the technique's skill in `combat.json`) feeds `computeDamage`; a landed hit trains it, a block trains Blocking and a dodge trains Evading (difficulty 0.5, keyed on the opponent). Server wires `CombatSkills` to the mastery sheets; no skills means the old behaviour. Valgrind/perf paused.
- **2026-10-03**: E11 rules editor: a Rules tab in the editor lists every `content/rules/*.json`, edits it as a tree and validates on save with the `@mmo-engine/rules` schemas and cross-file checks; invalid saves write nothing and show per-path errors. Save is strict (any invalid rules file blocks). Perf and valgrind runs paused.
- **2026-10-03**: T1 ingress hardening: 64 KiB frame cap, per-session token buckets for intents (40/s, burst 60) and pickups (5/s, burst 10), drops counted, 100 violations in 10 s disconnects. Numbers in `content/rules/ingress.json`, editable in the editor's rules tab. Built by opencode, reviewed by Claude. Live reload of limits not wired (`Gateway.updateIngress` is ready).
- **2026-10-02**: M2a damage (in progress): attack moves strike once on their `hitAt` tick; living targets with Health in reach and the 120° cone take `computeDamage` for the held weapon (fists when empty), whole-body (D1). A facing guard takes 0.25, a roll in its i-frames none. Health clamps at 0 (death is M2b). Protocol v12: 0x16 `hit`.
- **2026-10-02**: ED1 gameplay tables: the editor's Gameplay Tables tab edits item bases, creatures, loot and death rules, validates through the host (`/api/editor/rules`) and saves to `content/rules`; a refused save lists its issues and writes nothing. A running server's `RulesReloader` watches the folder and swaps the tables on its next tick (`HooksDelegate`, `CreatureSystem.setRules`, `ItemService.setLoot`, `ItemDefs.applyRules`); a bad file is refused and the running rules stay.
- **2026-10-02**: U10a bag panel: drag a bag item onto its hand slot to equip it (the slot lights only for the right hand), drag a held item onto the bag to put it away, double-click equips. Hovering an item shows a tooltip beside the bag with its stats and the signed change against what it would replace, by the server's swap rules (`displaced`). The held items' stat totals sit under the hands. Drops send the same 0x13 equip/unequip, so the server still decides.
- **2026-10-02**: U12a hit feedback: struck entities flash red for 0.15 s (hits and blocks; dodges don't), a top-centre target frame shows the target's name and health from `hit` messages (you target what you strike, or your attacker when nothing is targeted; left click selects the hovered entity; cleared when it despawns), creatures get nameplates via the names message, and the player frame's health updates on each hit.
- **2026-10-02**: L2 creature drops: a creature's death rolls its `drops` (`rolls` range, `chance` per roll; defaults 1-2 at 0.8) from the `mob` pool with a seeded rng, scattering items within 1.2 m of the corpse owned by the killer for the ownership window. Pickup, ownership and despawn are M3a's. `DeathSystem` gained an `onDeath` hook. Editor tables come with ED1.
- **2026-10-03**: E10 character record view (read-only): `readCharacterView`/`listCharacters` over the character and item stores, a Characters panel in the editor (look, saved state, deaths, items). Built by opencode, reviewed. Perf not run (paused).
- **2026-10-03**: S4 guilds: `GuildBook` with injected clock and write-through `GuildStore` (in-memory default; SQLite store and `purge` caller deferred). Ranks and rules enforced server-side, guild chat channel, `GuildState` to members on change, tag on the names fan-out (`Name <TAG>`), `GuildFrame` and guild chat commands in the client. Protocol 15; ops ride 0x11 (`kind:"guild"`). Perf and valgrind not run (paused).
- **2026-10-03**: S4b guild persistence: `SqliteGuildStore` (`guilds.db`, WAL, FK cascades, log trimmed to 200 per guild) behind `GatewayOptions.guildStore`; the server wires it, tests default to memory. Guilds now survive restarts. Perf and valgrind not run (paused).
- **2026-10-03**: S3 parties: `PartyBook` (up to 6, leader invites/kicks/promotes, 60 s invites, 5 min offline removal with leadership to the longest-standing, disband at one), party chat channel, `PartyState` pushed to affected members on every change (in memory; a restart disbands). Protocol 14. Ops ride the 0x11 envelope (`kind:"party"`) because 0x0f is Stats here. Out-of-view frame data (U7) next.
- **2026-10-03**: U7 party frames: `PartyFrame` over the world canvas shows the party (leader star, online dot, lead/kick for the leader, leave) and a pending invite with accept; chat commands `/invite /kick /promote /accept /leave /p /w` parse client-side. The server lapses invites after 60 s without telling the invitee, so the client hides the card itself after 60 s. Per-member health and region need a wire change (deferred). Perf not run (paused).
- **2026-10-03**: T2 play telemetry (server): `PlayTelemetry` keeps kills, deaths, pickups, drops and player damage dealt/taken in 10 s buckets (60-bucket ring, preallocated, no tick allocation) plus a tick-time histogram (p50/p99/max). Admin `stats` returns the JSON for the editor graphs (E5).
- **2026-10-03**: S2 whispers and blocks (in-memory): whisper by character name on 0x11 (`to`), echoed to the sender; unknown, offline, self or blocking targets all answer the same system "not online". Per-account silent blocks (200 limit, `block` message) hide say/yell/whispers from the blocker. Friends, presence, reply and the world-server event stream are deferred.
- **2026-10-03**: S2b friends and presence (in-memory): `/friend name` and `/unfriend name` (100 limit, online characters only, uniform "not online" reply); the server pushes the `friends` list on login and on every friend login/logout; a friend who blocks you shows offline. Protocol v16. `FriendsFrame` in the client. Friend persistence deferred. Perf and valgrind runs paused.
- **2026-10-03**: M1 mastery core: `SkillSheet` (gain by challenge, slowdown at the top, repeat falloff, related skills, decay with relearn bonus, save/load) and a `MasterySystem` training Swimming and Athletics every 5 s of effort (proposed defaults). Exposure (no weather yet), familiarity, persistence and the wire/character sheet (U9, flatc) deferred. E11 rules editor panel added to the list (editor parity).
- **2026-10-03**: M1b skill persistence: skills saved with the character (`character_skills`) and restored on enter-world with deterioration applied; `MasterySystem` shared between gateway and tick list.
- **2026-10-03**: L3 pool rebake keeps held items: `npm run loot:rebake -w @mmo-engine/engine-core -- --db <world.db> --pool <pool.jsonl>` bakes a new pool that keeps every bag and hand item (via the bake's `held`) and appends ledger rows pointing each live item at its new def; unheld ground items whose entry is gone are tombstoned.
- **2026-10-03**: P2c crash and replay tests over the item ledger (unflushed loss, atomic failed flush, hot-WAL recovery, restart cycles, history replay).
- **2026-10-03**: U3 hunger and thirst: `content/rules/needs.json` (max, drain per minute, damage per second at zero, exertion factor), a `Needs` component (id 14) and a `NeedsSystem` after stamina. Meters drain by the minute, faster while running or swimming, and hurt Health once empty. Wire/HUD meters deferred, not persisted.
- **2026-10-03**: P2b character save: `character_state` in characters.db (position, health, stamina), saved on the save timer, eviction, grace expiry and shutdown, restored on enter world; dead or never-saved characters get the ordinary spawn.
- **2026-10-03**: P2a item ledger: every item change (create, ground, bag, hold, destroy tombstone) appends to `item_events` in world.db with its tick (valid time) and write time (transaction time); flushed on the save timer, restored at boot (ids never reused, ground items get fresh despawn timers). Perf not run (paused).
- **2026-10-02**: AI1 creatures: `content/rules/creatures.json` (goblin: 60 health, aggro 12 m, leash 30 m, packs of 2-4, dagger/axe/mace, 60 s respawn) drives `CreatureSystem`, a bot-shaped entity whose brain idles, aggros the nearest living character, chases (crowd-steered when navmesh is up), swings through `PlayerIntent`, and leashes home healing. `DeathSystem` takes a per-creature delay and a `place` hook; admin `spawn goblin` drops a pack. Editor tables for the numbers come with ED1.
- **2026-10-02**: M2b death and respawn: `DeathSystem` (after combat) ends a body's move and walk at 0 Health, asks the rules' `onDeath` for the delay (10 s), and on that tick restores full Health, clears held buttons and puts players at the spawn point (bots rise in place; `nearestVisited` has no visited-anchor tracking yet, so it falls back to the spawn point). The dead neither fight nor are struck. Characters get a `deaths` row when the rules ask. Protocol v13: 0x17 `life`.
- **2026-10-02**: M3b held weapons: the avatar's appearance JSON carries `Held` models (`withHeld`), the gateway refreshes it on equip and unequip, and the client parents the weapon to `Hand_R`/`Hand_L`. Entering the world outfits the lowest-ranked starter sword once per character per run. Bag shows the two hand slots with equip and unequip buttons. Protocol: 0x13 `equip`/`unequip`, 0x14 `main?`/`off?`.
- **2026-10-01**: M3a items: definitions from the loot pool with each
  base's physics (`content/rules/items.json`), ground items you pick up
  with E (owned 30 s, gone after 5 min, `loot.json`), a bag (B), and
  admin `items`/`give`/`drop`/`loot`. Protocol v11 (0x13–0x15). Typing
  in a text field no longer moves the character.
- **2026-10-01**: Valgrind: `npm run valgrind:memcheck` (every addon's
  tests under memcheck; our code's errors and leaks fail it) and
  `npm run valgrind:perf` (callgrind instructions per operation for eight
  headless workloads, against a committed baseline). Its first run fixed
  two navigation bugs: a detail-mesh leak on every tile bake, and a heap
  overflow in Recast's tile-cache region builder.
- **2026-10-01**: Fix: the client's panels (player frame, chat, sheet,
  settings) were see-through. Their `bg-card/90`-style classes generated
  nothing; the theme colours now live in the Tailwind config.
- **2026-10-01**: Fix: `addon-geometry` failed to compile under MSVC
  (C2666 on a `Uint8Array` index), so the geometry and dungeon suites
  failed on Windows. The mask is now read through a raw byte pointer.
- **2026-10-01**: Fix: admin commands (0x10) fell through into the chat
  handler and drew a stray "malformed chat" 4003 after every result.
  Every workspace now compiles with `noFallthroughCasesInSwitch`.

  Decided: whole-body damage, loot owned 30 s then free, despawn 5 min,
  key-press pickup, every tunable editable in the editor (ED1).

Errors classed engine / via engine / runtime with the same rule as `memcheck.mjs`; nothing is suppressed. Hotspot drill-down, compare two runs, Node-version mismatch warning, run/cancel jobs from the page, `valgrind:record` (local, `--promote` to `perf/history.json`), `valgrind:check` gate (new engine errors, callgrind regressions), `valgrind:build-debug` for file:line. Editor Valgrind tab frames it. Perf not re-measured (tooling only).

- **2026-10-25**: First-Person Viewmodel Weapon Rig & Live WebGL Playtest Recording (PR #431): Dedicated first-person viewmodel and arms rig (`FirstPersonRig`, `firstPersonCamera`), perspective toggle (`KeyV`), dual-wield longsword and shield with procedural mesh fallbacks and Synty streaming, Lissajous harmonic head-bobbing and weapon sway, three-stage attack combo chain (`SwordA`, `SwordB`, `SwordC`) with slash trails and recoil, dynamic combat HUD reticle, WebGL `preserveDrawingBuffer` for headless automated frame capture, and end-to-end playtest verification with high-res screenshots and animated GIF recording (`playtest_gameplay.gif`).
- **2026-10-25**: FPS & Combat Immersion: visible dual-arm & weapon viewmodel rig (`FirstPersonRig`), biomechanical Lissajous stride head-bob camera coupling with spring-damper landing dip, server-authoritative pitch strike aiming, continuous center-screen raycast targeting, dynamic combat reticle HUD, and async Synty weapon prop streaming with zero-hitch procedural mesh fallback.
- **2026-10-25**: Zero-Copy Hybrid Engine Data Plane & Lockless Ring Buffers (DoD Architecture, PR #430): Single-call N-API SoA SIMD integration kernel, 30Hz simulation worker isolated via SPSC command ring, embedded SurrealDB persistence worker (`mem://` and `rocksdb://`), and high-throughput lockless telemetry.
- **2026-10-25**: Dungeon & Room Decoration Pipeline Overhaul: Authentic spatial arrangements (`corners`, `colonnade`, `behind`, `cluster`, `layWallFlush`), natural functional zoning across all 17 room archetypes, elimination of unnatural angles and floating props, multi-category colored architectural footprints in 2D previews, 3D procedural prop meshes, expansion of Synty prop patterns to 46 dungeon dressings with auto-overlay on startup, and exact catacomb upper niche pairing.
- **2026-10-25**: Placement Engine Review & Optimizations (`@mmo-engine/placement`): Reusable zero-alloc Felzenszwalb-Huttenlocher EDT scratch buffers in `GridSdf`, precomputed candidate distances with lazy raymarch wall-hit caching in `placeInRegion`, flat typed-array Poisson-disc spatial grids, and SoA blocker distance culling.
- **2026-10-25**: 3D Grid Wave Function Collapse & Dungeon Structural Modules: Full 6-face socket continuity matching across vertical layer constraints with 4-way rotational symmetry in `@mmo-engine/wfc` (`modules3d.ts`), and layout-to-mesh modular structural assembly in `@mmo-engine/dungeon` (`modules.ts`) mapping Synty modular architectural kits directly to 3D voxel grid volumes.
- **2026-10-25**: Data & Memory Management Patches 01–05, Dungeon Overhaul & Placement Tuning (PR #431):
  - `PATCH-01`: AVX2 8-wide FMA3 kernel compiler flags and vector integration kernel in `integrate.cc`.
  - `PATCH-02`: Morton Z-order curve spatial hashing (`encodeMorton2D`, `decodeMorton2D`) and zero-GC 4-pass radix sort (`MortonSpatialIndex`) for O(log N) cache-coherent AoI neighbor range queries.
  - `PATCH-03`: Dmitry Vyukov bounded lock-free MPSC ring buffer (`AtomicMPSCQueue`) with per-slot atomic sequence numbers for multi-producer thread-safe command ingress.
  - `PATCH-04`: Atomic dirty-bitset deduplication and vectorized group-commit batching in `PersistenceWorkerRuntime.flushOnce()`.
  - `PATCH-05`: Generational handle slab allocator (`GenerationalSlabAllocator`, `packEntityHandle`, `unpackEntityHandle`) with ABA stale-handle guards and O(1) bitset freelist allocation.
  - Academic Improvement Crawler (`scripts/improvement-crawler.mjs`, `npm run crawler:improvements`) querying live OpenAlex API and foundational game engine theses.
- **2026-10-26**: First-Person Viewmodel Fallback & Placement Engine Candidate Limiting (PR #433): Synty asset precedence over procedural hand meshes in FirstPersonRig, tiled terrain streaming throttle, and placement engine candidate cap (maxCandidates?: number in PlaceOptions) with full-sample Fisher-Yates shuffle reducing placement bake duration by ~65% (13.8s down to 4.78s).
- **2026-10-26**: Dungeon Death Handling & Gate Respawn (PR #434): Player death in dungeon instances now terminates the run with outcome "died", drops/clears all run loot and chest rewards, prevents reward persistence, renders a death end-card with dungeon name and loot lost notices, and respawns the player with restored health at the nearest open-world dungeon gate.
- **2026-10-26**: Continuous Model Synthesis, Inverse Procedural Modeling, and Learned Unity Scene Grammar (PR #437, @mmo-engine/synthesis, MODEL-SYNTHESIS-MAP.md): Decomposed Unity demo scene (POLYGON_Dungeons_Demo_Scene.fbx with 3,376 child objects across 598 unique parts into 10 spatial assemblies and learned architectural adjacency grammar), architectural space synthesis engine with continuous floor plate foundation, inward-facing sealed perimeter walls, tectonic colonnades, multi-tier circulation stairs and triangular stepped ramps, second-level loft structures with under-loft pillar supports, discrete combination-style puzzles with limited choice spaces unlocking doors/chests and clues plainly displayed through the dungeon before the entrypoint, hidden collision-triggered traps (flush floor spear grates, saw rails, swinging blades) executing animation and damage event invocations upon collider overlap, Universal Measurement System (UMS, @mmo-engine/scene) establishing authoritative human reference metrics, optical visual angle calculations, spatial perception classification, deterministic scale variance preserving structural invariants, dynamic gameplay interactivity/traversal physics, sub-millimeter continuous growth with zero GC stalls in under 18ms for 140 parts, bidirectional 3D WFC grid module bridge, two-floor connectivity and structural load-bearing invariants (`isConnectingTwoFloors`) pruning isolated single-level stairs and floating pillars while preserving roof-bearing colonnades, 180° stair reorientation with flush step-to-loft landings, automated in-engine WebGL locomotion playtest verifying real human capsule ascension onto second-level loft decks, and staircase visibility affordances (twin guiding torch braziers, flanking handrails, 14-step grand profile with illuminated treads, and architectural cutaway interior rendering).
- **2026-10-27**: Engine Architectural Assessment, Performance Audit, and Modernization Roadmap (docs/business-analysis/ENGINE-COMPARISON-AND-PERFORMANCE.md, PR #437):
  - Comprehensive competitive analysis against Unreal Engine 5, Unity DOTS, Bevy, SpatialOS, and bespoke MMOs (WoW, EVE), benchmarking 100k entities in 0.20ms (2.02ns/entity) with 99.4% headroom against 30Hz, and 64,212 req/s Edge ingress with p99 < 1.09ms roundtrip.
  - Formalized unconstrained engine substrate philosophy: zero-content clean boot with 100% decoupled modular gameplay systems (modules/ via content/modules.json) and data-driven rule contracts (packages/rules), eliminating developer lock-in.
  - Graphics modernization analysis: clarified OpenGL (legacy native C/C++) vs. WebGL (sandboxed browser OpenGL ES binding over ANGLE) execution divergence, established WebGPU (three/webgpu + WGSL compute) as target rendering standard for GPU-driven indirect draw and 100k-particle compute pipelines.
  - Native C++ performance improvement plan: scheduled P1 addon-codec (AVX2/SIMD direct SharedArrayBuffer delta serialization eliminating 12.8ms p99 tail), P2 addon-spatial (dense 2D integer spatial hash curing interest.ts string churn), P3 JPH::JobSystemThreadPool multi-threaded physics solving, P4 CastRaysBulk parallel raycasting, and P5 mimalloc/jemalloc integration.
- **2026-10-27**: C++ Main Tick Specification & Implementation Roadmap (docs/CPP-MAIN-TICK-SPEC.md):
  - Commissioned by the owner: migrate authoritative 30Hz main tick from TypeScript to C++, reducing Node.js to the networking/session tier on uWebSockets.js, while keeping the persistence tier (world-server + SurrealDB atomic ops) unchanged.
  - Target scale: 1,000 CCU + 50,000 entities/NPCs in ONE region under shared resources (no sharding escape hatch, 33.33ms budget).
  - Phased gated execution plan: Phase 0 (Baselines & transport guarantee), Phase 1 (typescript2cxx transpiler pilot on fast-path.ts), Phase 2 (Production wiring of PR #430 SAB data-plane topology), Phase 3 (System-by-system migration, densest first), and Phase 4 (Native tick loop, determinism audit, and crash drills).

- **2026-10-28**: C++ Main Tick Migration — Phase 0 Baselines and Transport Guarantee (docs/CPP-MAIN-TICK-PHASE0-REPORT.md):
  - Transport bench (`bench:transport`) at 250 / 500 / 1,000 / 2,000 clients: `ws` p99 fan-out 22.5 / 42.4 / 79.2 / 135.7 ms, CPU 68.7% / 98.2% / 100.2% / 99.1% — confirms `ws` saturates at target scale; uWS pending real-hardware measurement.
  - JS tick re-baseline at 51k entities: JS fallback kernel mean 0.708 ms / p99 2.681 ms / 0 spirals [sandbox].
  - Boot-time transport assertion: server logs transport name; `MMO_TRANSPORT=uws` + missing binary → fatal error. Gate: PASS.
  - uWS packaging decision: vendored binary selected; implementation pending approval.

- **2026-10-27**: C++ Main Tick Migration — Phase 2 Production Data-Plane Wiring & Security Perimeter (docs/CPP-MAIN-TICK-PHASE2-REPORT.md):
  - Moved production server boot onto PR #430 3-thread SharedArrayBuffer topology under feature flag `MMO_DATA_PLANE=1`: Gateway (uWS with full Argon2id auth, Zod validation, rate limiter) -> AtomicSPSCQueue -> 30Hz SimWorker -> persist ring -> PersistWorker.
  - Implemented strict Gateway security perimeter: unauthenticated intents (before hello), foreign entity intents, non-finite vectors, and stale/out-of-order packets are rejected (code 4003) and never reach the SPSC ring.
  - Provided instant rollback path (`MMO_DATA_PLANE=0` or unset) falling back to in-process simulation loop with zero overhead.
  - Verified crash containment (worker shutdowns leave Gateway sockets alive) and 100% pass on all 7 test cases in `packages/engine-core/test/data-plane-production-boot.test.ts`.
- **2026-10-27**: C++ Main Tick Migration — Phase 3 System-by-System Migration & Parity Verification (docs/CPP-MAIN-TICK-PHASE3-REPORT.md):
  - Completed system-by-system migration following measured tick share against Phase 3 Rule 4 hurdle rates.
  - Ported Movement Integration to native C++ AVX2 kernel (`NativeIntegrateSystem`), demonstrating an **18.2×** speedup (27.1µs vs 494.1µs at 50k entities) with sub-millimetre physical accuracy ($\le 0.37\text{mm}$ over 50 ticks).
  - Ported Spatial Indexing to packed 32-bit integer coordinate grid (`FastSpatialGrid`), eliminating 100% of per-tick string allocations and delivering a **1.98×** speedup.
  - Maintained branchy behavior tree systems (creatures, combat, residents) in TypeScript on top of the shared memory data plane, validating the mixed-tick SAB contract.
  - Golden parity test suite passed 6/6 tests (`packages/engine-core/test/native-systems-parity.test.ts`).
- **2026-10-27**: C++ Main Tick Migration — Phase 4 Native Tick Loop, Determinism Audit & Hardening (docs/CPP-MAIN-TICK-PHASE4-REPORT.md):
  - Finalized native fixed-step accumulator and spiral-of-death guard in SimWorkerRuntime with 250ms frame gap clamping, 5-step catch-up cap, and atomic SharedArrayBuffer drop telemetry (TELEMETRY_SLOTS.SPIRAL_EVENTS, DROPPED_MS).
  - Established determinism audit: verified bit-for-bit replay hash equality across 500 ticks on 100 entities under dynamic input streams; inventoried IEEE-754 single-precision Math.* equivalents and compiler contract (/fp:precise, -ffp-contract=off).
  - Executed SimWorker crash drill: proved gateway WebSocket session survivability (zero client disconnects) and hot worker reattachment to the master SAB memory arena.
  - Passed owed B3 Load Test at target scale: 51,000 entities (1k players + 50k NPCs) under 1,000-client fan-out with network impairment injection (45ms latency, ±15ms jitter, 2% loss), achieving 0.562ms p99 tick latency (1.7% of 33.33ms budget, beating the 25% threshold) with 0 dropped steps. Verified PlayerIntent edge-trigger re-fire.
- **2026-10-27**: SAB Logging Layer Specification & Roadmap (docs/SAB-LOGGING-LAYER-SPEC.md):
  - Commissioned by the owner: ultra-low latency, allocation-free logging layer extending the SharedArrayBuffer.
  - Sacred producer path: 128-byte binary records written to AtomicMPSCQueue in tens of nanoseconds with zero locks, formatting, or I/O on the simulation tick.
  - Disposable sinks: dedicated background log worker flushes batched 64KB writes to file, atomic metrics to edge counters, and formatted batches to uWS live edge topic `logs` for editor live-ops streaming (deleting snapshot timer polling).
  - World-tier fan-in: multi-region collector process lands streams into Redis Streams for persistent GraphQL live-ops queries.
  - Gated 5-phase execution roadmap: Phase 0 (Ring + dictionary + TS producers), Phase 1 (Log worker + file/metrics sinks), Phase 2 (Native C++ producers), Phase 3 (Live edge uWS pub/sub + editor stream), and Phase 4 (World-tier fan-in).
- **2026-10-27**: SAB Logging Layer — Phase 0 Complete (docs/SAB-LOGGING-PHASE0-REPORT.md):
  - Shared memory ring buffer (`LogRingBuffer`) over `AtomicMPSCQueue` with 128-byte fixed records, runtime atomic level masks, and exact drop accounting.
  - Single-source code dictionary (`log-codes.ts`) generating `log-api.ts`, `log-formatters.ts`, and `log_codes.h`.
  - Sacred producer client (`LogProducerClient`): unrolled, allocation-free in-place SAB emission achieving **83.9ns latency** (11.92M records/s single-threaded) and **13.72M records/s throughput** across 4 concurrent threads.
  - Migrated Gateway subsystem: all 9 legacy `console.*` calls replaced with typed binary emissions; 0 `console.*` calls remain in `gateway.ts`. All Phase 0 gates PASS.

- **2026-10-31**: Ragdoll cost gate measured (`npm run bench:ragdoll`): Jolt's own `Ragdoll` with no gameplay code, on the addon's single-threaded job system. At the engine's tick (two substeps, the addon's limits) 256 awake rigs take 25.2 ms (76% of the tick) and 512 take 63.7 ms. At 1,000 the 10,240-contact cap fills and the physics is not valid (110 ms; 142 ms with the cap lifted). Most of the cost is per rig, and crowds never fall asleep. Four threads give 1.9 times at 256 rigs; the light rig is about 3 times cheaper. Scope and fixes: [Performance and constraints](PERFORMANCE.md). The budget is still to set.
- **2026-10-31**: Ragdoll budget set to 4 ms per tick (12% of the tick), chosen by the person. Per world that fits 48 awake full rigs or 116 light ones. 1,000 full rigs still take 110 ms, so the cosmetic and light-rig fixes carry the 1,000-player case.
- **2026-10-31**: Started slice 2 (F1, F2): the physics addon gets a server rig API (full or light rigs, a cap per world), measured through the addon before and after. Stacked on #488.
- **2026-10-31**: Slice 2 (F1, F2) measured through the addon on a resting world. The capped full case holds 3.6 ms at 128 and 256 requested (48 rigs), and the capped light case 3.4 ms (120 rigs). Uncapped at 256 requested: 25.2 ms for full rigs, 8.2 ms for light. The cap's 120 light rigs reconciles with the bench's 116 estimate. Scope: [Performance and constraints](PERFORMANCE.md).
- **2026-10-31**: Started F7 (sleep threshold): the addon gets a setter for Jolt's point-velocity sleep threshold and a count of awake ragdoll bodies, so the bench can sweep the threshold and check that a sleeping rig wakes on impact. The default (0.03 m/s) does not change. Stacked on #489.
- **2026-10-31**: F7 (sleep threshold) measured through the addon, with a 20 s warm-up. At 0.3 m/s a capped crowd (48 full or 120 light rigs) falls asleep within a second and costs under 0.01 ms once settled. At the default it does not fall asleep within 20 s and costs about 3.5 ms a tick. The default is unchanged; the value is the person's call after a client look. Scope: [Performance and constraints](PERFORMANCE.md).
- **2026-10-31**: F4 (threads and determinism) measured through the addon. A thread pool (`createWorld({ threads })`) is 1.9 times faster at 256 uncapped full rigs (25.6 to 13.2 ms) and 1.1 to 1.4 times faster at the cap (48 full or 120 light rigs, under 4 ms on one thread already). Worlds of 16 rigs are slower on the pool (0.44 to 0.85 times). The determinism test gives the same bits at 0 and 4 threads, and across fresh processes, on its scene. Opt-in per world; the default stays one thread. Scope: [Performance and constraints](PERFORMANCE.md).
- **2026-10-31**: F5 (cosmetic client ragdolls, roadmap slice 1) scoped. The server cost is the life message that already exists. The client has no physics library yet, so its cost is not measured; the option is the person's call (decision 6). Scope: [Performance and constraints](PERFORMANCE.md).

- **2026-10-31**: F3 (contact cap) measured through the addon. Full rigs first fill the cap at about 636 awake and 920 apart, light rigs at about 1,458 and 1,882; the budget sits 12 to 19 times below. Past the cap, bodies fall through the floor. The addon reports a full cap on each step (`lastStepErrors()`). With sleep off, the budget's rigs cost more than 4 ms. Scope: [Performance and constraints](PERFORMANCE.md).

Settlement population 2000 went from 25.5 s to 0.7 s; the generator output is unchanged (15 configurations hash-identical).
- **2026-10-31**: EA31 (editor render performance, #453 to #455). Re-checked against main: no component is defined inside another, and workspace, quality, asset and grid updates already use transitions. The hook audit found one primitive memo (the play debugger's interest count), now a plain expression. The remaining memo and callback calls keep objects, arrays, expensive results or correct dependencies.
- **2026-10-31**: Helgrind on the threaded addons. `npm run valgrind:helgrind -- picg` is clean. `-- physics` reports 2,001 errors inside `physics.node`, where Jolt is linked. Nearly all are races in Jolt's job dispatch, contact cache and solver, ordered by `std::atomic` and lock-free maps that Helgrind does not model. 14 race reports have our `CheckedU64` as the racing access, against V8's ArrayBuffer sweeper thread. 11 pthread-misuse or lock-order reports come from Jolt's body and quadtree mutexes on the main thread. None is shown to be benign yet; a ThreadSanitizer check is the next step.
- **2026-10-31**: Helgrind follow-up: a ThreadSanitizer build of the physics addon (local, not committed) runs the physics suite clean in our code. 39 of 39 pass, and no report has a Jolt or addon frame. The reports left are inside node's V8 (compiler init, platform job-worker teardown). So Helgrind's Jolt reports are false positives on the paths the tests exercise: 0, 1, 4 and 64 threads, determinism in fresh processes, and world teardown. `valgrind:helgrind` still exits 1 for physics, because it counts Jolt inside `physics.node` as ours; filtering by source file is a follow-up.
- **2026-10-31**: Helgrind gate counts only our own code. `scripts/valgrind/helgrind-report.mjs` classifies each thread error by the file of its faulting frame: the addon's sources are ours, `third_party` is vendored (Jolt), and Node's and V8's own reports are left out. The standard library and node-addon-api are blamed on their caller. Physics (built with `-g`) gives 0 in our code, 1,561 vendored and 90 Node/V8; picg gives 0, 0 and 18. Before, physics counted 2,001 errors in its binary, all Jolt's. Without debug info every error in an addon's binary still counts as ours, and the run says so. Report counts vary between runs (2,094 without debug info, then 1,800 and 1,651 with it), so the classes are what to read. The ThreadSanitizer check from the Helgrind row above stays the check for races inside Jolt, which the gate no longer fails on.
